P44 — Hypothesis-bound fault injection and recovery challenge
PROPERTY_ID

P44

PROPERTY_NAME

Hypothesis-bound fault injection and recovery challenge

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Software fault injection, production failure testing and chaos-engineering practice.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANCE_LINEAGE

  2. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

  3. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

ORIGINAL_FORM

Empirically challenge a specific current failure/recovery claim under bounded, observable conditions.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Failure assumptions and runbooks remain untested, or chaos exercises become repetitive spectacle without decision consequence.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Real distributed failures depend on timing, topology, load, persistence and recovery interaction that unit tests miss.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Unknown failover/recovery behaviour, false confidence and discovery of critical coupling only during outage.

MECHANISM

State hypothesis/invariant; choose representative fault schedule/load; bound blast radius; predeclare evidence/stop conditions; test recovery/reconciliation; record decision.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Material claim about tolerance, failover, retry, restore, overload or state integrity whose assumptions can be safely challenged.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Static analysis/model checking/unit/integration test when the claim does not require distributed runtime failure; low-consequence system with no material uncertainty.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Representative environment/configuration, observability, rollback, safety authority, current topology, consequence assessment and independent expected result.

FAILURE_MODEL

Unexercised failure assumptions, non-representative injections, uncontrolled blast radius, missing observability and untested recovery.

TIMING_MODEL

Injection timing/order and stop conditions are part of the experiment.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

The tested invariant/postcondition is explicit; steady-state proxy alone is insufficient.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

Experiment authority, scope and rollback are bounded.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

Injected faults include loss, delay, duplicate/reorder where relevant.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

The experiment tests recovery and post-recovery reconciliation, not merely failure onset.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Predeclared evidence and abort conditions must be available.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Provides current empirical evidence and discovers integration faults outside formal or design models.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

System owner, operator, assurance reviewer and incident-response planner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Injected fault not representative

  2. No load during test

  3. Test stops before recovery

  4. Observability misses failure

  5. Blast radius escapes

  6. Repeated ritual yields no change

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Production experiments carry risk

  2. Staging may not reproduce common modes

  3. Evidence for broad effectiveness remains heterogeneous

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From random process killing to hypothesis-, invariant-, load-, recovery- and decision-bound experiments plus systematic schedule generation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A bounded experiment challenges one explicit claim and its recovery path; pass/fail changes evidence or action.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Run chaos experiments' is not a general property.

CURRENT_STATUS

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

P45 — Distribution requires a named consumer and retains a cheap local path
PROPERTY_ID

P45

PROPERTY_NAME

Distribution requires a named consumer and retains a cheap local path

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Repeated contrast between local atomicity and the costs introduced by RPC, partial failure, microservices and distributed transactions.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

  2. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Distribute only for a concrete need—failure independence, latency locality, scale, ownership/autonomy or separate lifecycle—and preserve the simpler non-trigger.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Remote boundaries are introduced because the architecture is fashionable rather than because they serve a requirement.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Every unnecessary boundary adds delay, unknown outcomes, version skew, partial failure, observability and recovery state.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Overengineering small/local problems and making correctness depend on infrastructure with no consumer.

MECHANISM

Name the consumer, quantify its need, compare local/single-owner alternative, and keep distribution out of paths where it adds no payoff.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Independent scaling, fault containment, geographic latency, ownership/autonomy or deployment lifecycle demonstrably outweighs coordination cost.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Co-locate code/state, use in-process calls and local transactions, or one durable owner.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Workload/consequence evidence, ownership model, availability/latency target and explicit cost/complexity comparison.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Preserves engineering value while stripping cloud-native fashion and accidental distribution.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect, product owner, operations and the team bearing lifecycle cost.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Future scale invoked without evidence

  2. Organisational boundary mistaken for runtime boundary

  3. Local path removed too early

  4. Remote API hides shared database/common mode

  5. Distribution consumer later disappears

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Needs can change

  2. Local design may create migration cost

  3. No universal threshold separates local from distributed

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From default networked decomposition to deliberate distribution with explicit trigger and reversible cheap path.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Every remote boundary names the property it buys, the assumptions it creates and the condition for collapsing it.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Microservices, queues, service mesh or consensus are not maturity indicators.

CURRENT_STATUS

STRONGLY_RETAINED

P46 — Retire distributed machinery when its coordination consumer disappears
PROPERTY_ID

P46

PROPERTY_NAME

Retire distributed machinery when its coordination consumer disappears

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Operational experience with long-lived middleware, consensus, queues and service decompositions.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

  2. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Coordination mechanisms are contingent means, not permanent institutions.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A queue, lock service, replica set, workflow, service boundary or global transaction remains after the invariant, scale or ownership need has vanished.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Obsolete machinery continues generating partial failure, upgrade, recovery and observability burden.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Ceremonial complexity, stale authority paths, unowned retries/backlogs and hidden single points of failure.

MECHANISM

Periodically restate consumer and trigger; measure use; plan drain/migration; preserve evidence and rollback; collapse to local/simple path when justified.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Mechanism has no current decision, invariant, workload or failure-domain consumer.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Keep mechanism only when removal would reintroduce a demonstrated failure or violate a current requirement.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Usage/dependency inventory, migration/replay plan, retained data/identity semantics and safe decommission evidence.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Prevents coordination debt from becoming an irreversible part of the system.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architecture owner, platform owner and operational teams.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Hidden client remains

  2. Old events need replay

  3. Removing quorum changes failure coverage

  4. Dual-write migration creates divergence

  5. No owner for retirement

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Retirement itself is risky

  2. Latent future need may return

  3. Benefits are hard to quantify before removal

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From infrastructure accumulation to lifecycle ownership with explicit retirement conditions and evidence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Each distributed mechanism has entry, operation and exit criteria; decommission preserves state and effect semantics.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Standard platform component' is not a permanent justification.

P47 — Microservices automatically improve resilience
PROPERTY_ID

P47

PROPERTY_NAME

Microservices automatically improve resilience

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Service-oriented and microservice architecture advocacy, contradicted by distributed coordination and integrity evidence.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

  2. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Decompose into independently deployable services and infer resilience from separation.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Deployment boundaries are mistaken for independent failure domains and semantic isolation.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Calls, data and retries cross more failure boundaries while dependencies and shared control planes remain coupled.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

This candidate prevents no general failure; it can create coordination debt unless real isolation/ownership is established.

MECHANISM

NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY. Evaluate each boundary for independent failure/deployment/ownership benefit, data integrity, retries, capacity and recovery.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Retain a service boundary only when it creates a real independently operable/failing/owned capability.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Modular monolith, local module or co-located state when independent runtime failure is unnecessary.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Independent data/authority, API semantics, capacity/isolation, end-to-end observability and recovery ownership.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Rejecting the automatic claim avoids architecture-by-brand while preserving useful service boundaries.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect, service owner and operations.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Shared database/control plane

  2. Chatty synchronous fan-out

  3. Cross-service invariant lost

  4. Retry cascade

  5. Distributed transaction/saga complexity

  6. Version skew

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Microservices can genuinely improve autonomy and fault containment

  2. Monoliths can also be fragile

  3. Evidence is context-sensitive

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From decomposition as default modernity to boundary-by-boundary evaluation and selective recombination.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A service boundary is justified by a named lifecycle, ownership, scale or failure-containment property and bears its distributed obligations.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The rejected proposition is itself a cloud-native caricature.

CURRENT_STATUS

  3. S076

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

P48 — A queue absorbs overload
PROPERTY_ID

P48

PROPERTY_NAME

A queue absorbs overload

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Simplified messaging/cloud architecture advice, contradicted by queueing limits and incident evidence.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. EVENT_LOG_AND_STREAMING_LINEAGE

  2. OVERLOAD_AND_SERVICE_RESILIENCE_LINEAGE

  3. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Insert a durable queue and treat acceptance as availability/capacity.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Buffering delays visibility of overload but cannot create service capacity; backlog age and recovery work accumulate.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Producer and consumer rates diverge under partial failure or demand spike.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

This candidate prevents no general overload failure; an unbounded queue can worsen it.

MECHANISM

NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY. Use P29–P33: bound queue, propagate backpressure, admit/shed, budget retries and control recovery surge.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

A queue is justified for decoupling, durability or burst smoothing only with a capacity/consequence model.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Synchronous backpressured call or small bounded local buffer.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Arrival/service rates, queue limits, deadlines, rejection semantics and drain capacity.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Rejects the false transformation of storage into throughput.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Service owner, queue operator and capacity planner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Disk exhaustion

  2. Expired work

  3. Head-of-line blocking

  4. Backlog recovery overload

  5. Caller retries accepted backlog

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Queues do absorb bounded bursts and decouple availability

  2. The problem is the unqualified claim, not queueing itself

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From buffering-as-resilience to bounded deferred work with backpressure and consequence policy.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Queue value is stated—durability, decoupling or bounded burst absorption—and completion capacity remains explicit.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Queue presence is not capacity.

CURRENT_STATUS

REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S045

  2. S062

  3. S063

  4. S066

  1. S073

EMPIRICAL_OR_OUTAGE_EVIDENCE

  1. S073

  2. S076

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

A bounded queue can provide real durability and temporal decoupling; it cannot manufacture downstream capacity.

P49 — Exactly-once delivery implies the business action occurs exactly once
PROPERTY_ID

P49

PROPERTY_NAME

Exactly-once delivery implies the business action occurs exactly once

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Transport/broker/stream-processing terminology promoted beyond its transactional boundary.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. MESSAGE_DELIVERY_LINEAGE

  2. EVENT_LOG_AND_STREAMING_LINEAGE

  3. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Treat an exactly-once processing/delivery feature as proof of one real-world effect.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Platform transactions close records, offsets or internal state but not arbitrary databases, APIs, payments, emails or physical actions.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

The external effect and platform commit fail independently; replies and retries remain ambiguous.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

This candidate prevents no general duplicate business effect outside the enclosed boundary.

MECHANISM

NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY. State the exact atomic closure; use semantic operation identity, idempotency, transaction participation, fencing or compensation for remaining effects.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Retain platform exactly-once semantics only for operations inside its documented transaction boundary.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

At-least-once plus semantic idempotency, or one local transaction, when simpler and sufficient.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Precise delivery/processing/effect boundary, operation identity, dedup horizon, external effect semantics and recovery.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Prevents vendor terms from silently widening into false business guarantees.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Application owner, broker/stream operator, workflow and business owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. External API called twice

  2. Offset committed separately

  3. Key expires

  4. Retry changes parameters

  5. Side effect irreversible

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Some integrated platforms genuinely provide exactly-once processing within scope

  2. Terminology is overloaded rather than always false

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From delivery slogan to boundary-qualified exactly-once processing/effect and explicit end-to-end closure.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Say exactly once only with the noun and boundary: record processing, state transition or verified effect under stated assumptions.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Unqualified 'exactly once' is ceremony.

P50 — Distributed lock alone makes concurrent mutation safe
PROPERTY_ID

P50

PROPERTY_NAME

Distributed lock alone makes concurrent mutation safe

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Distributed mutual exclusion and lock-service practice, refined by leases, epochs and fencing.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CONSENSUS_AND_MEMBERSHIP_LINEAGE

  2. FAULT_TOLERANCE_LINEAGE

  3. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Acquire a distributed lock and assume no other actor can mutate.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A delayed or paused former holder resumes after lease expiry or ownership transfer; the protected resource cannot distinguish it.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Lock service and protected resource have separate failure/timing domains.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

The lock alone does not prevent stale writes after handoff.

MECHANISM

Supersede with P13: monotone fencing generation enforced at every protected resource, or use semantic idempotency/conditional versioning.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

A lock may still coordinate efficiency or reduce contention, but correctness-critical mutation requires stale rejection.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Local mutex/local transaction, one durable writer, commutative operation or resource-native compare-and-swap.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Current lock/lease authority, generation, resource enforcement, pause/partition model and multi-resource closure.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Preserves useful coordination while removing unsafe reliance on client self-restraint.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Lock-service user, scheduler, storage/API owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. GC pause

  2. Lease expiry unnoticed

  3. Network partition

  4. External sink ignores token

  5. Lock token rolls back

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Some lock services integrate fencing/conditional writes

  2. Not every efficiency lock needs correctness-level fencing

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From mutual exclusion/lease ownership to resource-enforced generation and effect-boundary analysis.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Lock is advisory evidence; the mutation is safe because stale generations cannot take effect.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Lock acquired' is not proof of current mutation authority.

CURRENT_STATUS

P51 — Named infrastructure or repeated ritual as an engineering property
PROPERTY_ID

P51

PROPERTY_NAME

Named infrastructure or repeated ritual as an engineering property

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Cloud-native tooling, consensus folklore, event-sourcing fashion and chaos-engineering ritual.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

  2. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Install a consensus cluster, message broker, service mesh, workflow engine or chaos platform and infer the corresponding property.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Artefact presence substitutes for a protected invariant, failure model, evidence consumer and completion/recovery semantics.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Tooling adds new partial failures and dependencies while leaving the intended property unestablished.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY; the named artefact may help implement a property but prevents nothing by mere presence.

MECHANISM

Map artefact to property, assumptions, trigger, cheap path, evidence and retirement condition; remove or narrow when no consumer exists.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Only when the artefact implements a named property whose payoff exceeds its own lifecycle cost.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Local code/state, existing transactional store, simpler ownership or targeted test.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Property owner, invariant/consumer, failure model, integration evidence, operational capacity and exit plan.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Strips branding while retaining mechanisms that actually establish authority, delivery, recovery or resilience.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect, reviewer, operator and budget owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Dashboard-driven compliance

  2. Unused consensus service

  3. Event sourcing without replay semantics

  4. Chaos without recovery claim

  5. Service mesh hiding retry storms

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Standardised infrastructure can reduce implementation risk

  2. Shared platforms can amortise expertise

  3. Ceremony is contextual, not inherent in a tool

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From product/architecture labels to evidence-backed mechanism selection and lifecycle ownership.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Infrastructure is replaceable; the property, assumptions and evidence survive substitution.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

This property is explicitly the ceremony-stripping rule.

P52 — Byzantine/adversarial fault tolerance only under an explicit adversarial model
PROPERTY_ID

P52

PROPERTY_NAME

Byzantine/adversarial fault tolerance only under an explicit adversarial model

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Byzantine agreement and practical Byzantine state-machine replication.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANCE_LINEAGE

  2. CONSENSUS_AND_MEMBERSHIP_LINEAGE

  3. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

ORIGINAL_FORM

Replicate/agree despite arbitrary or malicious replica behaviour under authentication and threshold assumptions.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Crash-fault systems are described with Byzantine terminology without modelling compromised replicas, identities, cryptographic trust or correlated implementation faults.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Participants can send conflicting, malformed or strategically adversarial messages rather than merely stop.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Agreement/safety loss caused by up to the modelled number of arbitrary faulty participants.

MECHANISM

Use authenticated BFT protocol, 3f+1-style or protocol-specific threshold, independent administrative/failure domains, key/identity governance and deterministic validation.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Actual threat model includes mutually distrustful or compromise-prone participants and the cost is justified.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Crash-fault consensus, single authority, audited replication or simpler integrity checks when adversarial participants are not a credible failure.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Adversary/corruption model, authentication, identity/key lifecycle, independence, quorum threshold, denial-of-service/liveness assumptions and implementation assurance.

FAILURE_MODEL

Domain-specific crash, omission, arbitrary/value, model, scheduling and external-service failures.

TIMING_MODEL

Domain workload and latency model must be stated.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

Domain semantic validity remains separate from replicated agreement or durable execution.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

Agent/service/model/tool authority and version are explicit.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

Calls can be duplicated, omitted, delayed or return malformed/semantically invalid values.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

State/context, tool effects and human escalation are durable and reconcilable.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Prompt/model/tool version, causal provenance and validation evidence are captured within privacy/security limits.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Provides a distinct agreement property where arbitrary faults are genuinely in scope.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Adversarially exposed replicated service, multi-party system or high-consequence integrity owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Common software exploit compromises >f replicas

  2. Key theft collapses identity model

  3. DoS breaks liveness

  4. Clients/gateways outside model

  5. Economic/social adversary differs from protocol model

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. High cost/latency/complexity

  2. Often misapplied to ordinary common-mode failures

  3. Formal protocol does not secure implementation or operations

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From oral-message impossibility/thresholds to authenticated practical protocols and application-specific BFT designs.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

BFT is selected only after a concrete adversarial model and independent identity/failure-domain argument; crash/common-mode controls remain separate.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Byzantine' is not a synonym for severe outage.

CURRENT_STATUS

DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

PROPERTY_FAMILY

domain

P53 — Distributed AI and agentic systems as a bounded domain translation
PROPERTY_ID

P53

PROPERTY_NAME

Distributed AI and agentic systems as a bounded domain translation

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Multi-agent planning, agent workflows, tool-calling systems and recent attempts to apply sagas, validation and chaos testing.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

  2. WORKFLOW_ORCHESTRATION_LINEAGE

  3. CLOUD_NATIVE_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Treat agents/models/tools as independently failing distributed actors with durable context, operation identity, validation and effect reconciliation.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Probabilistic outputs, tool calls and context versions add semantic/value failure beyond crash/omission while familiar retry and workflow risks remain.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Agents, models, retrieval stores and tools can omit, duplicate, delay, hallucinate or semantically conflict while external effects persist.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Duplicate tools/effects, stale context, unvalidated plan execution, lost workflow state and false completion.

MECHANISM

Durable workflow state, versioned context/model/tool provenance, constrained authority, semantic validation, idempotent/fenced tool effects, compensation and targeted fault injection.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Autonomous or multi-agent system coordinates persistent work or external effects across independently failing services.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

One model call with human review and no durable/external effect; deterministic local program where suitable.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Agent/task identity, model/prompt/tool versions, validator authority, external effect closure, human escalation and privacy/security constraints.

FAILURE_MODEL

Domain-specific crash, omission, arbitrary/value, model, scheduling and external-service failures.

TIMING_MODEL

Domain workload and latency model must be stated.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

Domain semantic validity remains separate from replicated agreement or durable execution.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

Agent/service/model/tool authority and version are explicit.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

Calls can be duplicated, omitted, delayed or return malformed/semantically invalid values.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

State/context, tool effects and human escalation are durable and reconcilable.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Prompt/model/tool version, causal provenance and validation evidence are captured within privacy/security limits.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Transfers proven distributed-state disciplines while acknowledging probabilistic semantic uncertainty.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Agent-platform owner, workflow designer, tool/effect owner and human supervisor.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Validation shares model failure

  2. Context fork/staleness

  3. Tool call duplicated

  4. Agent retries amplify cost/load

  5. Model update changes replay

  6. Goal completion self-reported

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Evidence is very recent and mostly prototypes/preprints

  2. Anthropomorphic 'agent' framing can obscure ordinary workflow design

  3. Semantic correctness lacks stable oracle

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Current translation is moving from chat/session memory to durable, validated, transaction-like agent workflows and fault injection.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Provisional: agentic execution is governed as a durable distributed workflow, but probabilistic semantic validity is not promoted to a solved systems property.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Multi-agent' does not imply distributed-systems maturity.

CURRENT_STATUS

UNRESOLVED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

LOW

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S092

  2. S097

  3. S098

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S055

  2. S056

EMPIRICAL_OR_OUTAGE_EVIDENCE

[]

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Formal or implementation correctness does not establish that the deployed assumptions, external effects and operational boundary satisfy the claim.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

  1. Whether agent-specific reliability mechanisms form genuinely new general properties remains unresolved.

  2. Operational field evidence and independent replication are sparse.

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH_PARTITION
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

LOW

FORMAL_OR_THEORETICAL_STRENGTH

MEDIUM

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT

  1. Which agent/tool effects can duplicate or remain unknown after retry?

  2. What validates semantic correctness independently of the agent reporting success?

  3. Can model/prompt/context versions reproduce or explain an action?

PROPERTY_FAMILY

P54 — Default global consensus or coordination for all distributed state
PROPERTY_ID

P54

PROPERTY_NAME

Default global consensus or coordination for all distributed state

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Consensus folklore and overgeneralisation from strong consistency/transactions.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CONSENSUS_AND_MEMBERSHIP_LINEAGE

  2. DISTRIBUTED_DATABASE_LINEAGE

  3. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

ORIGINAL_FORM

Put every state transition through one global order or distributed lock because distribution exists.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Coordination is used without a non-confluent invariant or current-authority consumer.

DISTRIBUTED_FAILURE_MODE

Global quorum/leader becomes latency, availability and throughput dependency; failure domain expands.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

No general failure is prevented when operations commute, partition safely, tolerate staleness or can be locally owned.

MECHANISM

NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY. Start with P14/P45; use P16 for safe autonomy and P15 only for the minimal non-confluent invariant.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Strong coordination triggers only for an explicit invariant/current authority that cannot be preserved by local ownership, partitioning, commutativity, escrow or compensation.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Local transaction, single writer, CRDT/invariant-confluent operation, partitioned ownership or asynchronous durable handoff.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Invariant analysis, operation algebra, failure/latency target, membership and recovery evidence.

FAILURE_MODEL

Ceremonial architecture can introduce partial failure, coordination debt and unowned recovery without a protected consumer.

TIMING_MODEL

No general timing model; the claim fails because a named artefact is substituted for an engineering property.

CONSISTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No guarantee follows from branding alone.

MEMBERSHIP_OR_AUTHORITY_PRECONDITIONS

No authority follows from deployment labels alone.

DELIVERY_OR_IDEMPOTENCY_PRECONDITIONS

No delivery/effect semantics follow from tooling labels alone.

RECOVERY_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery evidence follows from having a component installed.

OBSERVABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Dashboards and green checks are proxies unless tied to a claim.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Avoids global bottlenecks and availability loss while retaining coordination where it actually buys correctness.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Application/data architect and invariant owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

  1. Global leader bottleneck

  2. Quorum loss stops unrelated work

  3. Cross-region latency

  4. Huge reconfiguration blast radius

  5. Lock ordering deadlock

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

  1. Under-coordination can silently violate invariants

  2. Simplicity of one order can outweigh cost at modest scale

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

From one-size-fits-all strong ordering to invariant-scoped coordination and hybrid systems.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Coordinate only the state/effect closure whose independent execution would violate the named invariant.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

'Distributed means consensus' is the rejected caricature.

CURRENT_STATUS

REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. S025

  2. S027

  3. S038

  4. S039

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. S011

  2. S036

  3. S040

EMPIRICAL_OR_OUTAGE_EVIDENCE

  1. S073

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Global ordering can be the simplest correct choice at modest scale when a central invariant dominates; the rejected element is defaulting without that consumer.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

  1. How can teams reliably discover hidden non-confluent invariants before removing coordination?

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH_PARTITION

  1. Which invariant requires a global order?

  2. Could local ownership, commutativity, partitioning, escrow or compensation preserve it more cheaply?
